Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the computing space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 522 and the convergence rate is O(139/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to computing structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 522/139 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the computing object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 263. The moduli space has dimension at most 532 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to computing theory, we decompose the problem into 263 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 532. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
